{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T235337Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\nFollow this workflow; do not treat a green build or successful upload as proof that the intended app shipped.\n\n## Start with the requested stack\n\nWhen the user wants Scaffold-ETH 2 or has not selected a stack for a conventional contract-plus-frontend dApp, use its generator:\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@2.0.23 # Tested version; update deliberately\n```\n\nUse the generated Foundry/Next.js monorepo, wallet integration, contract hooks, and components. Respect an explicitly requested alternative stack.\n\n## Choose the local chain deliberately\n\n- Use `yarn chain` for isolated contracts, mocks, and unit tests.\n- Use `yarn fork --network <chain>` when behavior depends on deployed protocols, tokens, balances, or other real chain state.\n- In fork mode, point the frontend at the local Anvil network (`chains.foundry`, chain ID 31337), not the upstream chain being copied. Switch to the real target chain only for a real deployment.\n\nA fork also grants powers the real chain does not. Fund demo identities from state that already exists rather than deploying a mock token:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount <whale>\ncast send <token> \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <demo-account> <amount> \\\n  --from <whale> --unlocked\n```\n\n`anvil_setBalance` and `anvil_setStorageAt` are equivalent overrides when no suitable holder exists. Nothing here is broadcast; the fork is a local copy.\n\nAnvil normally mines only when a transaction arrives, so between transactions the latest block and `block.timestamp` remain frozen; the next transaction advances time in one jump. This silently breaks live deadlines, expiry, and vesting displays even when `vm.warp` unit tests pass. For continuous behavior, enable interval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nFor a frozen live demo, enable interval mining. Mining a single block with `evm_mine` restamps `block.timestamp` once and it freezes again immediately; manual mining and `evm_increaseTime` are for controlled single-step tests, not a running demo. Add `--block-time 1` to the fork script when the project should always run that way.\n\n## Build a static IPFS release\n\nConfigure the IPFS build for static export, route directories, and static images:\n\n```typescript\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\nSet the production origin before building so Open Graph URLs and images do not resolve to localhost. Then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\nOn Node 25, the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the standard Web Storage methods when no backing file is configured; libraries detect it and crash on calls such as `getItem()` during static prerender. Apply a process-level remedy inherited by build workers, not only code in `instrumentation.ts` or `next.config.ts`. Depending on the app, use one of:\n\n```bash\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\"\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n```\n\n## Verify before and after upload\n\nBefore uploading, confirm the expected change is in `out/`, that every route emitted its own directory, and that generated metadata carries the production origin:\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html # One directory with an index.html per route\n```\n\nAfter uploading:\n\n1. Record the CID. An unchanged CID means the uploaded bytes are identical; check the build output and upload target before blaming gateway caching.\n2. Load the root and at least one non-home route through the gateway. Root success does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\" # Expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. Verify the Open Graph image and URL use the production origin.\n4. For ENS, update the content hash only after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved, then confirm the resolver and public gateway serve that CID.\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until these checks pass.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"21cb7e73-94dd-46e8-b190-4a46c2697c6b","uuid":"b07ff056-e9b9-46c4-a568-82186d2c7d0a","timestamp":"2026-08-19T23:53:42.658Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WsxAoHC5XjE5tfoTMUw7aH","type":"tool_result","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"./DeployHelpers.s.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/YourContract.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice Deploy script for YourContract contract\n * @dev Inherits ScaffoldETHDeploy which:\n *      - Includes forge-std/Script.sol for deployment\n *      - Includes ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier\n *      - Provides `deployer` variable\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol  # local anvil chain\n * yarn deploy --file DeployYourContract.s.sol --network optimism # live network (requires keystore)\n */\ncontract DeployYourContract is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /**\n     * @dev Deployer setup based on `ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT` in `.env`:\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-default\": Uses Anvil's account #9 (0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720), no password prompt\n     *      - \"scaffold-eth-custom\": requires password used while creating keystore\n     *\n     * Note: Must use ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner modifier to:\n     *      - Setup correct `deployer` account and fund it\n     *      - Export contract addresses & ABIs to `nextjs` packages\n     */\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        new YourContract(deployer);\n    }\n}\n===HELPERS===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Script, console } from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport { Vm } from \"forge-std/Vm.sol\";\n\ncontract ScaffoldETHDeploy is Script {\n    error InvalidChain();\n    error DeployerHasNoBalance();\n    error InvalidPrivateKey(string);\n\n    event AnvilSetBalance(address account, uint256 amount);\n    event FailedAnvilRequest();\n\n    struct Deployment {\n        string name;\n        address addr;\n    }\n\n    string root;\n    string path;\n    Deployment[] public deployments;\n    uint256 constant ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE = 10000 ether;\n\n    /// @notice The deployer address for every run\n    address deployer;\n\n    /// @notice Use this modifier on your run() function on your deploy {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD1egDQNz3duxmRChb6t","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015CuJb4NwfEmH3HQ1Hu9Xv9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T235337Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\n/**\n * @notice Minimal interface for the ERC-20 functions the tip jar relies on.\n */\ninterface IERC20 {\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256);\n}\n\n/**\n * @title TipJar\n * @notice Accepts USDC tips and keeps an onchain feed of them.\n * @dev USDC is an ERC-20 with 6 decimals. Tippers must `approve` this contract\n *      for the USDC amount first, then call `tip(...)`. The tipped USDC is held\n *      by this contract until the owner withdraws it.\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    /// @notice The ERC-20 token accepted as tips (Base USDC).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // who sent the tip\n        uint256 amount; // amount in USDC base units (6 decimals)\n        string message; // optional message attached to the tip\n        uint256 timestamp; // block timestamp when the tip landed\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, oldest first.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of USDC received across all tips (base units).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    event NewTip(uint256 indexed index, address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error TransferFailed();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @param _usdc Address of the USDC token contract.\n     * @param _owner Address that may withdraw collected tips.\n     */\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = _owner;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), _owner);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @dev Requires the caller to have approved this contract for `amount` USDC.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in base units (6 decimals, e.g. 1_000_000 == 1 USDC).\n     * @param message Optional message to store alongside the tip.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        bool ok = usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n\n        uint256 index = tips.length;\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipsCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Return the most recent `count` tips, newest first.\n     * @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n     */\n    function recentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 len = tips.length;\n        if (count > len) count = len;\n\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            out[i] = tips[len - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice USDC currently held by the jar (base units).\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Withdraw all collected USDC to the owner.\n     */\n    function withdraw() external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (bal == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        bool ok = usdc.transfer(owner, bal);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n\n        emit Withdraw(owner, bal);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Transfer ownership to a new address.\n     * @param newOwner The address that should become the owner.\n     */\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1574,"cache_read_input_tokens":20727,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1574},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"21cb7e73-94dd-46e8-b190-4a46c2697c6b","uuid":"7fe5c287-b986-4b42-9ac1-2d3182193e27","timestamp":"2026-08-19T23:56:56.286Z","request_id":"req_011CeD1efFN7X1eoDLGxQTEV"}

Override with the USDC_ADDRESS env var if needed.\n *\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /// @notice Base USDC (also valid on a Base fork).\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", BASE_USDC);\n        // The deployer becomes the owner who can withdraw collected tips.\n        TipJar tipJar = new TipJar(usdc, deployer);\n        deployments.push(Deployment({ name: \"TipJar\", addr: address(tipJar) }));\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1969,"cache_read_input_tokens":22301,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1969},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"21cb7e73-94dd-46e8-b190-4a46c2697c6b","uuid":"34a70488-30fa-49af-8992-b999ee8215c3","timestamp":"2026-08-19T23:57:08.011Z","request_id":"req_011CeD1g8wmdn6fMTLS7Z8HU"}

When running against a Base fork\n *      (chain id 31337) that same address is present because the fork copies\n *      Base state. Override with the USDC_ADDRESS env var if needed.\n *\n * Example:\n * yarn deploy --file DeployTipJar.s.sol\n */\ncontract DeployTipJar is ScaffoldETHDeploy {\n    /// @notice Base USDC (also valid on a Base fork).\n    address constant BASE_USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;\n\n    function run() external ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner {\n        address usdc = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", BASE_USDC);\n        // The deployer becomes the owner who can withdraw collected tips.\n        TipJar tipJar = new TipJar(usdc, deployer);\n        deployments.push(Deployment({ name: \"TipJar\", addr: address(tipJar) }));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD1jntt72os6QXAKqPVf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0176mNG2h82VhiUSswiWi9s2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T235337Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/tip-jar/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: UNLICENSED\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Minimal 6-decimal ERC-20 mock standing in for USDC in unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[from][msg.sender] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar tipJar;\n\n    address owner = address(0xABCD);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        tipJar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100e6);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(tipJar), amount);\n        tipJar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testTipRecordsFeedAndTransfersUsdc() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(tipJar.tipsCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(tipJar.totalTipped(), 5e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(tipJar)), 5e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95e6);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = tipJar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5e6);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testRecentTipsAreNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2e6, \"second\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = tipJar.recentTips(10);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].from, bob);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"second\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].from, alice);\n    }\n\n    function testTipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        tipJar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testOnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"thanks\");\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(tipJar)), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":402,"cache_read_input_tokens":29267,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":402},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"21cb7e73-94dd-46e8-b190-4a46c2697c6b","uuid":"5029576a-9223-4a13-a0af-af25f65aa1e6","timestamp":"2026-08-19T23:58:01.575Z","request_id":"req_011CeD1jmuqv1S8yBBMipTnc"}

in unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        balanceOf[msg.sender] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[from][msg.sender] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar tipJar;\n\n    address owner = address(0xABCD);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n    address bob = address(0xB0B);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        tipJar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100e6);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(tipJar), amount);\n        tipJar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function testTipRecordsFeedAndTransfersUsdc() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(tipJar.tipsCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(tipJar.totalTipped(), 5e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(tipJar)), 5e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 95e6);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = tipJar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5e6);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testRecentTipsAreNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2e6, \"second\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = tipJar.recentTips(10);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].from, bob);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"second\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].from, alice);\n    }\n\n    function testTipRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        tipJar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testOnlyOwnerWithdraws() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"thanks\");\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(tipJar)), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testWithdrawRevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        tipJar.withdraw();\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD1yY2p3fEPS2TncCYgS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XZxohHafaRQoD4aaj59Lt9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T235337Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/tip-jar/packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { HeartIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\ntype Tip = {\n  from: string;\n  amount: bigint;\n  message: string;\n  timestamp: bigint;\n};\n\nconst formatUsdc = (amount?: bigint) =>\n  amount === undefined ? \"0\" : Number(formatUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n\nconst timeAgo = (timestamp: bigint) => {\n  const seconds = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp));\n  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s ago`;\n  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)}m ago`;\n  if (seconds < 86400) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 3600)}h ago`;\n  return `${Math.floor(seconds / 86400)}d ago`;\n};\n\nconst StatCard = ({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) => (\n  <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center bg-base-100 rounded-xl px-6 py-4 shadow\">\n    <span className=\"text-3xl font-bold\">{value}</span>\n    <span className=\"text-sm opacity-70\">{label}</span>\n  </div>\n);\n\nconst TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { data: tipJarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const tipJarAddress = tipJarInfo?.address;\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJarAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    try {\n      if (!amount) return 0n;\n      return parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const handleSend = async () => {\n    if (!connectedAddress) {\n      notification.error(\"Connect your wallet first\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter an amount greater than zero\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      setIsSending(true);\n\n      // Step 1: approve the tip jar to move USDC, if the current allowance is too low.\n      if (allowance === undefined || allowance < parsedAmount) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [tipJarAddress, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Step 2: send the tip.\n      await writeTipJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD29q143TT49t5Vhb2gE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016kpVKzRuuVLZG9YEgFus26","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T235337Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/tip-jar/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA small dApp that accepts **USDC tips on Base** and shows a live onchain feed of\nthem. Built on [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js).\n\nIt has three parts:\n\n- **`TipJar.sol`** — an onchain contract that pulls USDC from the tipper\n  (`transferFrom`) and stores each tip (`from`, `amount`, `message`, `timestamp`)\n  in an onchain feed. The owner can withdraw the collected USDC.\n- **A web page** — a tip feed, a form to send a tip (amount + optional message),\n  running stats (total tipped, number of tips, jar balance), and a\n  connect-wallet flow (RainbowKit).\n- **Local dev against a Base fork** — the app runs against a local Anvil fork of\n  Base, so the **real** Base USDC contract\n  (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`) is available at its real\n  address without deploying a mock.\n\n> Nothing here is deployed to a live network. The deliverable is the working\n> local project. To go live you would deploy `TipJar` to Base (8453) and point\n> the frontend's target network at Base — see [Going to real Base](#going-to-real-base).\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Node.js](https://nodejs.org) (>= 20)\n- [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com) (v3, comes with the repo via Corepack)\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`)\n\n## Setup\n\nYou'll want **three terminals** (fork, deploy, frontend). All commands run from\nthe project root unless noted.\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\n```\n\n### 2. Start a local Base fork (terminal 1)\n\nThis forks Base mainnet into a local chain with id `31337`. `--block-time 1`\nkeeps blocks (and the feed's timestamps) advancing every second.\n\n```bash\nanvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1\n```\n\nLeave this running. It exposes an RPC at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n\n> Any Base RPC URL works in place of `https://mainnet.base.org` (e.g. an Alchemy\n> or Infura Base endpoint) and is more reliable for heavier use.\n\n### 3. Deploy the TipJar contract (terminal 2)\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis deploys `TipJar` to the fork, wiring it to Base USDC, and auto-generates the\nABI + address into `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deployer\n(Anvil account #9) becomes the contract owner who can withdraw tips.\n\n### 4. Start the frontend (terminal 3)\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**.\n\nThe frontend is already configured to target the local fork\n(`chains.foundry`, id `31337`) in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\n## Sending a tip\n\n1. **Connect a wallet.** The simplest option is the built-in **burner wallet**\n   (click _Connect Wallet_ → it's the default on the local chain). To use\n   MetaMask instead, add a network with RPC `http://127.0.0.1:8545` and chain id\n   `31337`, and import an Anvil test key.\n2. **Get some test USDC.** A fresh wallet has no USDC. On the fork you can pull\n   USDC from any real Base holder by impersonating them. Replace `<YOUR_ADDRESS>`\n   with your connected wallet address:\n\n   ```bash\n   RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\n   USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n   WHALE=0x20FE51A9229EEf2cF8Ad9E89d91CAb9312cF3b7A   # a large Base USDC holder\n\n   cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\n   cast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC   # gas for the whale\n   # Send 100 USDC (6 decimals) to your address:\n   cast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <YOUR_ADDRESS> 100000000 \\\n     --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\n   ```\n\n3. **Fill in the form** (amount in USDC + an optional message) and click\n   **Send tip**. The first tip triggers **two prompts**: a one-time USDC\n   `approve`, then the `tip` transaction. The feed and stats update on their own.\n\n## Running the contract tests\n\nUnit tests use a mock USDC token, so they don't need the fork:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/foundry\nforge test -vv\n```\n\n## Project layout\n\n- `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` — the tip jar contract\n- `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` — deploy script (USDC address configurable via `USDC_ADDRESS`)\n- `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` — unit tests\n- `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` — the tip jar page (feed + form + stats)\n- `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` — registers Base USDC for the frontend\n\n## Going to real Base\n\nThis project intentionally stays local. To deploy for real you would:\n\n1. Add a keystore account with funds and run `yarn deploy --network base`.\n2. Add `chains.base` to `targetNetworks` in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`\n   and register USDC under chain id `8453` in `externalContracts.ts`.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":612,"cache_read_input_tokens":60581,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":612},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"21cb7e73-94dd-46e8-b190-4a46c2697c6b","uuid":"8746c95a-d4b6-41b6-ab29-f876b43dd281","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:03:52.610Z","request_id":"req_011CeD29pBSJmviNX6thTURE"}

Start a local Base fork (terminal 1)"," ","+This forks Base mainnet into a local chain with id `31337`. `--block-time 1`","+keeps blocks (and the feed's timestamps) advancing every second.","+","+```bash","+anvil --fork-url https://mainnet.base.org --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1"," ```","-yarn chain","-```"," ","-This command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.","+Leave this running. It exposes an RPC at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`."," ","-3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:","+> Any Base RPC URL works in place of `https://mainnet.base.org` (e.g. an Alchemy","+> or Infura Base endpoint) and is more reliable for heavier use."," ","-```","+### 3. Deploy the TipJar contract (terminal 2)","+","+```bash"," yarn deploy"," ```"," ","-This command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.","+This deploys `TipJar` to the fork, wiring it to Base USDC, and auto-generates the","+ABI + address into `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deployer","+(Anvil account #9) becomes the contract owner who can withdraw tips."," ","-4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:","+### 4. Start the frontend (terminal 3)"," ","-```","+```bash"," yarn start"," ```"," ","-Visit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+Open **http://localhost:3000**."," ","-Run smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`","+The frontend is already configured to target the local fork","+(`chains.foundry`, id `31337`) in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`."," ","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`","+## Sending a tip"," ","+1. **Connect a wallet.** The simplest option is the built-in **burner wallet**","+   (click _Connect Wallet_ → it's the default on the local chain). To use","+   MetaMask instead, add a network with RPC `http://127.0.0.1:8545` and chain id","+   `31337`, and import an Anvil test key.","+2. **Get some test USDC.** A fresh wallet has no USDC. On the fork you can pull","+   USDC from any real Base holder by impersonating them. Replace `<YOUR_ADDRESS>`","+   with your connected wallet address:"," ","-## Documentation","+   ```bash","+   RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545","+   USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913","+   WHALE=0x20FE51A9229EEf2cF8Ad9E89d91CAb9312cF3b7A   # a large Base USDC holder"," ","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","+   cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC","+   cast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xde0b6b3a7640000 --rpc-url $RPC   # gas for the whale","+   # Send 100 USDC (6 decimals) to your address:","+   cast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <YOUR_ADDRESS> 100000000 \\","+     --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC","+   ```"," ","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","+3. **Fill in the form** (amount in USDC + an optional message) and click","+   **Send tip**. The first tip triggers **two prompts**: a one-time USDC","+   `approve`, then the `tip` transaction. The feed and stats update on their own."," ","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","+## Running the contract tests"," ","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","+Unit tests use a mock USDC token, so they don't need the fork:"," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+```bash","+cd packages/foundry","+forge test -vv","+```","+","+## Project layout","+","+- `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` — the tip jar contract","+- `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` — deploy script (USDC address configurable via `USDC_ADDRESS`)","+- `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` — unit tests","+- `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` — the tip jar page (feed + form + stats)","+- `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` — registers Base USDC for the frontend","+","+## Going to real Base","+","+This project intentionally stays local. To deploy for real you would:","+","+1. Add a keystore account with funds and run `yarn deploy --network base`.","+2. Add `chains.base` to `targetNetworks` in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`","+   and register USDC under chain id `8453` in `externalContracts.ts`."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

</div>\n);\n\nconst TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { data: tipJarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2VtfsZ97cmQbs1Q52z","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The whole thing is built and running locally. Here's what shipped in `tip-jar/`:\n\n## What was built\n\n**Contract — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`**\n- `tip(amount, message)` pulls USDC via `transferFrom` and appends `{from, amount, message, timestamp}` to an onchain feed\n- View helpers: `recentTips(n)` (newest-first), `tipsCount()`, `totalTipped()`, `balance()`\n- Owner-only `withdraw()` and `transferOwnership()`, custom errors, and `NewTip`/`Withdraw` events\n- **5 unit tests pass** (`forge test`), using a mock USDC so they run without a fork\n\n**Web page — `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`**\n- **Tip feed** (address + amount + message + time-ago), auto-refreshing each block\n- **Send-tip form** (amount in USDC + optional message) that does the two-step USDC `approve` → `tip` flow, with balance checks and notifications\n- **Stats row** (total tipped / tip count / jar balance) and the **RainbowKit connect-wallet** flow from the header\n\n**Local run against a real Base fork** (per the frontend playbook — behavior depends on real USDC, so I forked rather than mocked):\n- Anvil forks Base (`--chain-id 31337 --block-time 1` for live timestamps), so the **real** Base USDC at `0x8335…2913` is present at its real address\n- `TipJar` deployed to the fork (owner = Anvil #9), ABI auto-exported to the frontend; USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts`\n- Frontend already targets `chains.foundry` (31337)\n\n## Verification\n- Foundry tests: 5/5 pass · TypeScript: clean · ESLint: clean (0 errors/warnings) · Home page: HTTP 200, renders the tip jar UI\n- **End-to-end proof on the fork**: impersonated a Base USDC whale → funded a demo wallet → approved + tipped 5 USDC with a message. The contract now reads `tipsCount = 1`, `totalTipped = 5 USDC`, `jarBalance = 5 USDC`. (That one demo tip, \"gm from cast,\" is sitting in the live feed as evidence — it'll clear if you restart the fork.)\n\n**README.md** documents prerequisites and the three-terminal flow (`anvil` fork → `yarn deploy` → `yarn start`), plus how to grab test USDC by impersonating a whale, and a \"going to real Base\" note.\n\n## Currently running\n- Anvil Base fork — `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n- Next.js dev server — `http://localhost:3000`\n\nNothing was deployed to any live network. Want me to stop the background fork/dev-server processes, or leave them up for you to poke at?"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1025,"cache_read_input_tokens":74349,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1025},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"21cb7e73-94dd-46e8-b190-4a46c2697c6b","uuid":"f03d3c0c-54b9-40cf-aab5-33ed149b0f4b","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:07:58.377Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2VsgLVQwkSTtQZ6oPn"}
